- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `server/services/skill_drafts.py`:

```python
"""Saving a skill from chat (0.1.61 §C7): a draft in skill_candidates and a "Save as a skill /
Not now" card; nothing is saved until the person clicks (promote/reject).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

from server.services import skill_files, skill_forge

MAX_FILES = 20
MAX_FILE_BYTES = 100_000
MAX_TOTAL_BYTES = 1_000_000
_CARD_BODY = 4000


def key_for(name: str) -> str:
    from server.services import skill_import          # the one slug (Global Constraints)
    return skill_import.slug(name or "") or "skill-" + hashlib.sha256((name or "").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8]


def check_files(raw) -> dict[str, str]:
    """The draft's files: {relative path: text}, inside the skill, at most 20, 100 KB each, 1 MB
    in all; SKILL.md is made from the draft itself."""
    if raw in (None, {}, []):
        return {}
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError("files must be an object of {relative path: text}")
    if len(raw) > MAX_FILES:
        raise ValueError(f"at most {MAX_FILES} files")
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    total = 0
    for rel, text in raw.items():
        clean = skill_files.safe_relpath(str(rel))
        if clean is None or clean == "SKILL.md" or not isinstance(text, str):
            raise ValueError(f"not a file of the skill: {rel!r}")
        size = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if size > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
            raise ValueError(f"{clean} is larger than {MAX_FILE_BYTES // 1000} KB")
        total += size
        out[clean] = text
    if total > MAX_TOTAL_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("the files are larger than 1 MB together")
    return out


async def propose(args: dict, *, conversation_id: str | None, emit) -> dict:
    """The model's save_skill: a draft and a card, or why not (nothing shown)."""
    name = " ".join(str(args.get("name") or "").split())[:100]
    description = " ".join(str(args.get("description") or "").split())[:500]
    body = str(args.get("body") or "").strip()
    try:
        files = check_files(args.get("files"))
        cand = await skill_forge.create_candidate(
            key=key_for(name), name=name, category="saved", description=description, body=body,
            source="chat", conversation_id=conversation_id, files=files or None)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"ok": False, "error": str(exc),
                "note": "Nothing was shown to the user. Fix it and call save_skill again."}
    emit({"type": "skill_proposed", "candidate_id": cand.id, "key": cand.key, "name": name,
          "description": description, "body": body[:_CARD_BODY], "files": sorted(files)})
    return {"ok": True, "proposed": True, "candidate_id": cand.id,
            "note": "Shown to the user as a card (Save as a skill / Not now). Nothing is saved until they click."}
```

`server/services/skill_forge.py`. Add to the imports: `import hashlib`, `import yaml`, and
`from server.services import capability_scan, skill_files, skill_ledger`.
- **`_held_by_own`.** Add above `create_candidate`:

```python
async def _held_by_own(db, key: str) -> bool:
    """Whether one of Arslan's own skills holds `key`. A skill read from a shared folder does not:
    Arslan's own takes its name, and the shared copy is listed as not used (Ruling 8)."""
    from server.services.skill_import import EXTERNAL_ORIGINS   # skill_import imports this module
    pack = await db.get(SkillPack, key)
    return pack is not None and pack.origin not in EXTERNAL_ORIGINS
```
- **`create_candidate`.** Add the keyword parameters `conversation_id: str | None = None` and
  `files: dict | None = None`, and pass both to `SkillCandidate(...)`. Its live-key check
  `if await db.get(SkillPack, key) is not None:` becomes `if await _held_by_own(db, key):`. The
  docstring's first line becomes "Validate + insert a new SkillCandidate (status='observing') —
  from the Forge, create_skill, or save_skill (0.1.61 §C7: with the conversation and its files)."
- **`promote_candidate`, `reject_candidate` and the new helpers.** Replace `promote_candidate`
  and `reject_candidate` with:

```python
class _Decided(Exception):
    """Someone else decided this draft in the meantime."""


def _skill_md(name: str, description: str, body: str) -> str:
    front = yaml.safe_dump({"name": name, "description": description}, allow_unicode=True,
                           sort_keys=False).strip()
    return f"---\n{front}\n---\n\n{body.strip()}\n"


async def promote_candidate(candidate_id: int) -> dict:
    """The person's "Save as a skill" (or the Forge's promote): a live SkillPack, origin "saved".
    A draft's files become its folder with a generated SKILL.md; it is scanned (dangerous: saved
    off); a draft from a conversation writes one ledger row."""
    async with db_session.AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        cand = await db.get(SkillCandidate, candidate_id)
        if cand is None:
            return {"ok": False, "reason": "candidate not found"}
        if cand.status not in _NON_TERMINAL:
            return {"ok": False, "reason": f"already {cand.status}"}
        if await _held_by_own(db, cand.key):
            return {"ok": False, "reason": f"a live skill with key '{cand.key}' already exists"}
        key, name, category, description, body = cand.key, cand.name, cand.category, cand.description, cand.body
        files, conversation_id = dict(cand.files or {}), cand.conversation_id
    skill_md = _skill_md(name, description, body)
    aside = None
    if files:
        staged = skill_files.stage_texts(files, skill_md=skill_md)
        try:
            scan = capability_scan.scan_skill_folder(staged.path, skill_md)
            aside = skill_files.swap_in(staged, key)
        except BaseException:
            skill_files.discard(staged)
            raise
    else:
        scan = capability_scan.scan_texts(skill_md, {})
    enabled = scan["verdict"] != "dangerous"
    try:
        async with db_session.AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
            cand = await db.get(SkillCandidate, candidate_id)
            if cand.status not in _NON_TERMINAL or await _held_by_own(db, key):
                raise _Decided()
            shared = await db.get(SkillPack, key)
            if shared is not None:                     # a shared-folder skill had the name: Arslan's own wins
                await db.delete(shared)
                await db.flush()
            db.add(SkillPack(key=key, name=name, category=category, description=description, tier="safe",
                             status="registered", body=body, origin="saved",
                             folder=str(skill_files.folder_for(key)) if files else None,
                             text_sha256=hashlib.sha256(skill_md.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
                             scan=scan, enabled=enabled))
            cand.status = "promoted"
            cand.promoted_at = datetime.utcnow()
            await db.commit()
    except _Decided:
        if files:
            skill_files.swap_back(key, aside)
        return {"ok": False, "reason": "already decided"}
    except BaseException:
        if files:
            skill_files.swap_back(key, aside)
        raise
    skill_files.retire(aside)
    if conversation_id:
        await skill_ledger.record(target=f"skill:{key}", detail=name, allowed=True,
                                  conversation_id=conversation_id)
    return {"ok": True, "key": key, "enabled": enabled, "scan": scan}


async def reject_candidate(candidate_id: int) -> dict:
    """Mark a candidate 'rejected' (terminal): the person's "Not now" on a chat draft is written to
    the ledger as a no (§A8)."""
    async with db_session.AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        cand = await db.get(SkillCandidate, candidate_id)
        if cand is None:
            return {"ok": False, "reason": "candidate not found"}
        cand.status = "rejected"
        key, name, conversation_id = cand.key, cand.name, cand.conversation_id
        await db.commit()
    if conversation_id:
        await skill_ledger.record(target=f"skill:{key}", detail=name, allowed=False,
                                  conversation_id=conversation_id)
    return {"ok": True}


async def check_candidate(candidate_id: int) -> dict:
    """0.1.61 §C7: the Forge's check without an expert — the validation and the scan every skill
    gets. The ReplayGate needs an expert's scored runs, so it runs only when one is picked.
    Passing moves an observing draft to proposed; the person's Save is still the gate."""
    async with db_session.AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        cand = await db.get(SkillCandidate, candidate_id)
        if cand is None:
            return {"ok": False, "reason": "candidate not found"}
        if cand.status != "observing":
            return {"ok": False, "reason": f"candidate is {cand.status}"}
        errors = validate(cand.key, cand.name, cand.description, cand.body)
        scan = capability_scan.scan_texts(_skill_md(cand.name, cand.description, cand.body), dict(cand.files or {}))
        dangerous = scan["verdict"] == "dangerous"
        passed = not errors and not dangerous
        rules = ", ".join(f["rule"] for f in scan["findings"] if f["level"] == "high")
        if errors:
            reason = "; ".join(errors)
        elif dangerous:
            reason = f"the scan found: {rules}"
        else:
            reason = "valid and scanned" + (" (with notes)" if scan["verdict"] == "caution" else "")
        cand.evidence = {"passed": passed, "reason": reason, "scan": scan}
        if passed:
            cand.status = "proposed"
        status = cand.status
        await db.commit()
    return {"ok": True, "status": status, "gate": {"passed": passed, "reason": reason, "aggregate": None}}
```

```tsx
/** 0.1.61 §C4: a license Arslan does not recognise shows its full text before anything is imported;
 *  "Use it on this Mac only" sends the text's sha256; the server can still ask (409). */
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor, cleanup } from "@testing-library/react";

vi.mock("react-i18next", () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (k: string, o?: Record<string, unknown>) => (o ? `${k}:${Object.values(o).join("|")}` : k) }),
}));
vi.mock("../api/discovery", () => ({ scanSkills: vi.fn(), importSkill: vi.fn() }));

import SkillImportPanel from "../components/SkillImportPanel";
import * as discovery from "../api/discovery";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";

const COMMIT = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567";
const GPL = "GNU GENERAL PUBLIC LICENSE\nVersion 3, 29 June 2007";
const SHA = "a".repeat(64);
const SKILL = { path: "SKILL.md", key: "handoff", name: "handoff", description: "Hand work over.", files: 2, bytes: 10,
  has_scripts: true, update: false, importable: true, reason: null,
  license: { spdx: "GPL-3.0", recognised: false, read_from: "LICENSE", sha256: SHA, text: GPL } };
const RESULT = { repo: { full_name: "example-org/example-skills", html_url: "", license: "GPL-3.0", stars: 3 },
  commit: COMMIT, subpath: "", more: 0, skills: [SKILL] };
const DONE = { key: "handoff", name: "handoff", description: "d", update: false, commit: COMMIT, local_only: true,
  recognised: false, license: "GPL-3.0", enabled: true, has_scripts: true, skipped: [], changes: null };

afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.clearAllMocks(); });

async function scanned(result: unknown = RESULT) {
  vi.mocked(discovery.scanSkills).mockResolvedValue(result as never);
  render(<SkillImportPanel />);
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText("capabilities.import.repo_placeholder"),
                   { target: { value: "example-org/example-skills" } });
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByText("capabilities.import.scan"));
  await screen.findByTestId("scanned-SKILL.md");
}

describe("importing a skill whose license Arslan does not recognise", () => {
  it("shows the whole text first, and imports only after 'Use it on this Mac only'", async () => {
    await scanned();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("license-unrecognised-SKILL.md")).toBeInTheDocument();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("import-SKILL.md"));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("license-text")).toHaveTextContent("GNU GENERAL PUBLIC LICENSE");
    expect(discovery.importSkill).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    vi.mocked(discovery.importSkill).mockResolvedValue(DONE as never);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("license-use"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(discovery.importSkill).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      `example-org/example-skills@${COMMIT}`, "SKILL.md", SHA));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("import-done-SKILL.md")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.localOnly");
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("license-step")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("Cancel imports nothing", async () => {
    await scanned();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("import-SKILL.md"));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("license-cancel"));
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("license-step")).toBeNull();
    expect(discovery.importSkill).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a recognised license imports at once, pinned to the scanned commit", async () => {
    await scanned({ ...RESULT, skills: [{ ...SKILL, license: { spdx: "MIT", recognised: true, read_from: "repo", sha256: SHA } }] });
    vi.mocked(discovery.importSkill).mockResolvedValue({ ...DONE, local_only: false, recognised: true } as never);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("import-SKILL.md"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(discovery.importSkill).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      `example-org/example-skills@${COMMIT}`, "SKILL.md", undefined));
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("license-step")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("when the server asks (an update whose text changed), the step shows the server's text", async () => {
    await scanned({ ...RESULT, skills: [{ ...SKILL, update: true }] });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("import-SKILL.md")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.update");
    vi.mocked(discovery.importSkill).mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError("license_step", 409, {
      code: "license_step", key: "handoff", name: "handoff",
      license: { spdx: "GPL-3.0", recognised: false, read_from: "LICENSE", sha256: "b".repeat(64), text: GPL + "\nAmended." } }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("import-SKILL.md"));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("license-text")).toHaveTextContent("Amended.");
    vi.mocked(discovery.importSkill).mockResolvedValue({ ...DONE, update: true,
      changes: { added: [], removed: [], changed: ["SKILL.md"], files_changed: 1, skill_md_diff: "+new line" } } as never);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("license-use"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(discovery.importSkill).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(
      `example-org/example-skills@${COMMIT}`, "SKILL.md", "b".repeat(64)));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("import-done-SKILL.md")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.updated:1");
  });
  it("says before the click when the import takes the name of a shared-folder skill", async () => {
    await scanned({ ...RESULT, skills: [{ ...SKILL, license: { spdx: "MIT", recognised: true, read_from: "repo", sha256: SHA },
      replaces: { source: "agents", folder: "/Users/someone/.agents/skills/handoff" } }] });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("replaces-SKILL.md")).toHaveTextContent("/Users/someone/.agents/skills/handoff");
  });
});
```

`web/src/api/discovery.ts`. Replace the `ScannedSkill`, `SkillScanResult`, `ImportedSkill` and

```ts
/** A skill's license as the server read it (0.1.61 §C4). `text` comes when it is not recognised:
 *  the person reads it and decides; `sha256` is what "Use it on this Mac only" sends back. */
export type SkillLicenseInfo = {
  spdx: string | null; recognised: boolean; read_from: string; sha256: string; text?: string;
};

export type ScannedSkill = {
  path: string;
  key?: string;
  name?: string;
  description?: string;
  body_bytes?: number;
  invocable?: boolean;
  files: number;
  bytes: number;
  has_scripts: boolean;
  /** Importing it again re-pins it (§C2): the button says Update. */
  update: boolean;
  /** The shared-folder skill whose name this import takes (Ruling 8), said before the click. */
  replaces?: { source: string; folder: string | null } | null;
  license?: SkillLicenseInfo;
  importable: boolean;
  reason: string | null;
};

export type SkillScanResult = {
  repo: { full_name: string; html_url: string; license: string | null; stars: number };
  /** The commit the scan read; the import pins to it. */
  commit: string;
  subpath: string;
  skills: ScannedSkill[];
  more: number;
};

export type ImportedSkill = {
  key: string; name: string; description: string; update: boolean; commit: string;
  replaces: { source: string; folder: string | null } | null;
  local_only: boolean; recognised: boolean; license: string | null; enabled: boolean; has_scripts: boolean;
  skipped: string[];
  changes: { added: string[]; removed: string[]; changed: string[]; files_changed: number; skill_md_diff: string } | null;
};

export const importSkill = (ref: string, path: string, licenseOk?: string) =>
  request<ImportedSkill>("/discovery/skills/import", {
    method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ ref, path, ...(licenseOk ? { license_ok: licenseOk } : {}) }),
  });
```

Keep `scanSkills` as it is. Update the section comment above them to: `// ── 0.1.61 §C2/§C4:
import a skill whole (verbatim, pinned); an unrecognised license is the person's decision ──`.

Create `web/src/lib/licenseStep.ts` (both import clients use it):

```ts
import type { SkillLicenseInfo } from "../api/discovery";

/** The server's license step (0.1.61 §C4): an import answered 409 `license_step` carries the text
 *  the person must read. Read by shape (status and detail), so it works whatever module made the
 *  error — and in tests that mock `api/client` without `ApiError`. */
export function licenseStepOf(e: unknown): { name?: string; license: SkillLicenseInfo } | null {
  const err = e as { status?: number; detail?: { code?: string; name?: string; license?: SkillLicenseInfo } } | null;
  if (!err || err.status !== 409 || err.detail?.code !== "license_step" || !err.detail.license) return null;
  return { name: err.detail.name, license: err.detail.license };
}
```

```ts
/** 0.1.61 §C1/§C4/§C6/§C7: the skills list, the license step and the skill proposal. Six languages, same keys. */
const en = {
  source_bundled: "Built in", source_imported: "Imported", source_saved: "Yours",
  source_agents: "Shared skills folder (~/.agents/skills)", source_claude: "Claude Code (~/.claude/skills)",
  source_codex: "Codex (~/.codex/skills)",
  sourcePath: "Read from {{path}}; Arslan never changes it.", sourceMissing: "{{path}} is not on this Mac.",
  shadowed: "Not used, the name is taken: {{names}}",
  hasScripts: "Has scripts", localOnly: "Local use only, not redistributable", hidden: "Used only when you name it",
  scanCaution: "Note: {{rules}}", scanDangerous: "Off: its scan found {{rules}}", readWhy: "Why",
  turnOnAnyway: "Turn on anyway", pin: "Pin to the top of Arslan's list", unpin: "Unpin",
```

`web/src/i18n.ts`. Add `import { skillLibraryMessages } from "./locales/skillLibrary";` after the
other locale imports. Then register it in the six resource lines with this one-off script, run
from the worktree root:

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path("web/src/i18n.ts")
s = p.read_text()
for lang in ("en", "zh", "ja", "es", "de", "fr"):
    old = f"ui: uiMessages.{lang} }}"
    assert s.count(old) == 1, lang
    s = s.replace(old, f"ui: uiMessages.{lang}, skillLibrary: skillLibraryMessages.{lang} }}")
p.write_text(s)
EOF
```

Create `web/src/components/skills/LicenseStep.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import type { SkillLicenseInfo } from "../../api/discovery";
import { Button } from "../kit";

/**
 * The license step (0.1.61 §C4, the user's decision 4): a license Arslan does not recognise is
 * shown in full — as plain text, never rendered — and the person decides. "Use it on this Mac
 * only" sends the text's sha256; the server compares it with the text it fetched itself.
 */
export default function LicenseStep({ name, license, busy = false, onUse, onCancel }: {
  name: string; license: SkillLicenseInfo; busy?: boolean; onUse: (sha256: string) => void; onCancel: () => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <div data-testid="license-step" role="group" aria-label={t("skillLibrary.licenseTitle")}
      className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-3 py-2.5">
      <p className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.licenseTitle")}</p>
      <p className="text-[12px] text-subtle-foreground">
        {name}{license.read_from !== "none" ? ` · ${t("skillLibrary.licenseFrom", { where: license.read_from })}` : ""}
      </p>
      <pre data-testid="license-text"
        className="max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-background p-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-foreground">
        {license.text ?? ""}
      </pre>
      <p className="text-[12px] text-subtle-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.licenseLocalOnly")}</p>
      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" tone="plain" data-testid="license-cancel" onClick={onCancel}>{t("skillLibrary.licenseCancel")}</Button>
        <Button size="sm" tone="primary" data-testid="license-use" disabled={busy} onClick={() => onUse(license.sha256)}>
          {t("skillLibrary.licenseUse")}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Replace `web/src/components/SkillImportPanel.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { FileDown, RefreshCcw, Check, X, Shield, FileCode2, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { scanSkills, importSkill, type ImportedSkill, type ScannedSkill, type SkillLicenseInfo,
  type SkillScanResult } from '../api/discovery';
import { licenseStepOf } from '../lib/licenseStep';
import LicenseStep from './skills/LicenseStep';

type Step = { path: string; name: string; license: SkillLicenseInfo };

/**
 * SkillImportPanel (0.1.61 §C2, §C4): import a skill whole, pinned to the scanned commit. A
 * license Arslan does not recognise is shown in full first; the server re-checks it (409). An
 * import that takes the name of a shared-folder skill says so before the click.
 */
export default function SkillImportPanel({ onImported }: { onImported?: () => void } = {}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [ref, setRef] = useState('');
  const [subpath, setSubpath] = useState('');
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<SkillScanResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busyPath, setBusyPath] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [imported, setImported] = useState<Record<string, ImportedSkill>>({});
  const [importErr, setImportErr] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step | null>(null);

  const scan = async () => {
    if (!ref.trim()) return;
    setScanning(true); setError(null); setResult(null); setImported({}); setImportErr({}); setStep(null);
    try {
      setResult(await scanSkills(ref.trim(), subpath.trim()));
    } catch (e) {
      setError(String(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e));
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  };

  const doImport = async (s: ScannedSkill, licenseOk?: string) => {
    if (!result) return;
    if (!licenseOk && !s.update && s.license && !s.license.recognised) {
      setStep({ path: s.path, name: s.name ?? s.path, license: s.license });   // read it first (§C4)
      return;
    }
    setBusyPath(s.path);
    setImportErr((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: '' }));
    try {
      const out = await importSkill(`${result.repo.full_name}@${result.commit}`, s.path, licenseOk);
      setImported((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: out }));
      setStep(null);
      onImported?.();
    } catch (e) {
      const asked = licenseStepOf(e);                // the server's own text (an update whose text changed)
      if (asked) {
        setStep({ path: s.path, name: asked.name ?? s.name ?? s.path, license: asked.license });
      } else {
        setImportErr((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: String(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e) }));
      }
    } finally {
      setBusyPath(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <p className="text-[10.5px] font-mono text-subtle-foreground select-none">{t('capabilities.import.subtitle')}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={ref}
          onChange={(e) => setRef(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !scanning) void scan(); }}
          placeholder={t('capabilities.import.repo_placeholder')}
          className="flex-1 bg-background border border-border/60 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-foreground font-mono placeholder-subtle-foreground"
        />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={subpath}
          onChange={(e) => setSubpath(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={t('capabilities.import.subpath_placeholder')}
          className="w-40 bg-background border border-border/60 focus:border-primary focus:outline-none rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-foreground font-mono placeholder-subtle-foreground"
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void scan()}
          disabled={scanning || !ref.trim()}
          className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-surface/90 hover:bg-border border border-border-strong text-foreground text-[10.5px] font-bold font-mono uppercase rounded-lg flex items-center gap-1 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        >
          {scanning ? <RefreshCcw className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> : <FileCode2 className="w-3 h-3" />}
          <span>{t('capabilities.import.scan')}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 bg-danger/15 border border-danger/40 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-danger font-sans">
          <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 mt-0.5" /> <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {result && (
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10.5px] font-mono">
            <span className="text-foreground font-bold">{result.repo.full_name}</span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-raised text-subtle-foreground">
              <Shield className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />{result.repo.license ?? t('capabilities.import.no_license')}
            </span>
            <span className="text-subtle-foreground">{result.commit.slice(0, 12)}</span>
          </div>
          {result.skills.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground font-sans">{t('capabilities.import.none_found')}</p>
          )}
          {result.skills.map((s) => {
            const done = imported[s.path];
            return (
              <div key={s.path} data-testid={`scanned-${s.path}`}
                className="bg-background border border-border/60 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <span className="text-[11.5px] font-bold text-foreground">{s.name ?? s.path}</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-subtle-foreground">{t('skillLibrary.files', { count: s.files })}</span>
                      {s.has_scripts && (
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-mono bg-primary/10 text-primary px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('skillLibrary.hasScripts')}</span>
                      )}
                      {s.license && !s.license.recognised && (
                        <span data-testid={`license-unrecognised-${s.path}`}
                          className="text-[9px] font-mono bg-warning/15 text-warning px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('skillLibrary.licenseUnrecognised')}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {s.description && <p className="text-[10.5px] text-subtle-foreground font-sans mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">{s.description}</p>}
                    {s.replaces && !done && (
                      <p data-testid={`replaces-${s.path}`} className="text-[10.5px] text-warning font-sans mt-0.5">
                        {t('skillLibrary.replacesShared', { folder: s.replaces.folder ?? s.replaces.source })}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {(importErr[s.path] || (!s.importable && s.reason)) && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-danger font-sans mt-0.5">{importErr[s.path] || s.reason}</p>
                    )}
                    {done && (
                      <p data-testid={`import-done-${s.path}`} className="text-[10.5px] text-success font-mono mt-0.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        {done.update ? t('skillLibrary.updated', { count: done.changes?.files_changed ?? 0 }) : t('capabilities.import.imported')}
                        {done.local_only && <><Lock className="w-3 h-3" />{t('skillLibrary.localOnly')}</>}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {done?.changes?.skill_md_diff && (
                      <details className="mt-1">
                        <summary className="text-[10px] text-subtle-foreground cursor-pointer">{t('skillLibrary.diff')}</summary>
                        <pre className="text-[10px] font-mono whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-48 overflow-auto">{done.changes.skill_md_diff}</pre>
                      </details>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {!done && (
                    <button
                      type="button" data-testid={`import-${s.path}`} onClick={() => void doImport(s)}
                      disabled={!s.importable || busyPath === s.path}
                      className="px-3 py-1 bg-primary/10 hover:bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30 text-primary text-[10px] font-mono uppercase rounded-lg transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      {busyPath === s.path ? <RefreshCcw className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> : <FileDown className="w-3 h-3" />}
                      <span>{s.update ? t('skillLibrary.update') : t('capabilities.import.import')}</span>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
                {step?.path === s.path && (
                  <LicenseStep name={step.name} license={step.license} busy={busyPath === s.path}
                    onUse={(sha) => void doImport(s, sha)} onCancel={() => setStep(null)} />
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {result.more > 0 && <p className="text-[10.5px] text-subtle-foreground">{t('skillLibrary.more', { count: result.more })}</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  const doImport = async (s: ScannedSkill, licenseOk?: string) => {
    if (!scan) return;
    if (!licenseOk && !s.update && s.license && !s.license.recognised) {
      setLicenseAsk({ path: s.path, name: s.name ?? s.path, license: s.license });   // read it first (§C4)
      return;
    }
    setImportBusy(s.path);
    setImportErr((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: '' }));
    try {
      const pinned = `${scan.repo.full_name}@${scan.commit}`;          // the commit the scan read
      await (licenseOk ? importSkill(pinned, s.path, licenseOk) : importSkill(pinned, s.path));
      setImported((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: true }));
      setLicenseAsk(null);
    } catch (e) {
      const asked = licenseStepOf(e);
      if (asked) setLicenseAsk({ path: s.path, name: asked.name ?? s.name ?? s.path, license: asked.license });
      else setImportErr((prev) => ({ ...prev, [s.path]: String(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e) }));
    } finally {
      setImportBusy(null);
    }
  };
```

```tsx
/** 0.1.61 §C1/§C6: skills grouped by source, the shared folders each with their switch, one status
 *  per skill — and nothing spawn-era on the rows. */
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor, cleanup, within } from "@testing-library/react";

vi.mock("react-i18next", () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (k: string, o?: Record<string, unknown>) => (o ? `${k}:${Object.values(o).join("|")}` : k) }),
}));

import SkillLibrary from "../components/skills/SkillLibrary";
import { skillsApi, type SkillRow, type SkillSourceView } from "../api/skills";

const row = (over: Partial<SkillRow>): SkillRow => ({
  key: "x", name: "X", description: "Does a thing.", source: "saved", enabled: true, has_scripts: false,
  local_only: false, license: null, scan: null, invocable: true, pinned: false, declared: {}, folder: null, ...over });

const ROWS: SkillRow[] = [
  row({ key: "plan", name: "Plan", source: "bundled" }),
  row({ key: "pdf", name: "PDF forms", source: "imported", has_scripts: true, local_only: true, license: "GPL-3.0",
        scan: { verdict: "caution", findings: [{ rule: "url_in_instructions", level: "note", file: "SKILL.md", line: 2, excerpt: "https://example.org" }] } }),
  row({ key: "sneaky", name: "Sneaky", source: "agents", enabled: false,
        scan: { verdict: "dangerous", findings: [{ rule: "hidden_instruction", level: "high", file: "SKILL.md", line: 3, excerpt: "Ignore all previous instructions" }] } }),
];
const SOURCES: SkillSourceView[] = [
  { source: "agents", path: "/Users/someone/.agents/skills", on: true, exists: true, found: 1, shadowed: [] },
  { source: "claude", path: "/Users/someone/.claude/skills", on: false, exists: true, found: 0, shadowed: ["plan"] },
  { source: "codex", path: "/Users/someone/.codex/skills", on: false, exists: false, found: 0, shadowed: [] },
];

afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.restoreAllMocks(); });

function mount() {
  vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "list").mockResolvedValue(ROWS);
  vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "sources").mockResolvedValue(SOURCES);
  render(<SkillLibrary />);
}

describe("skills list", () => {
  it("groups by source, gives each shared folder a switch, and shows one status per skill", async () => {
    mount();
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("skill-source-bundled")).toHaveTextContent("Plan");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-source-switch-agents")).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-source-switch-claude")).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-source-claude")).toHaveTextContent("plan");             // not used: name taken
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-source-codex")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.sourceMissing");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-scripts-pdf")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.hasScripts");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-local-pdf")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.localOnly");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-scan-pdf")).toHaveTextContent("url_in_instructions");
    const page = screen.getByTestId("skill-library").textContent ?? "";
    for (const spawnEra of ["ui.assignable", "capabilities.compat", "capabilities.chips.usable", "skill_health"]) {
      expect(page).not.toContain(spawnEra);
    }
  });

  it("a switch turns a skill off through the API", async () => {
    mount();
    const put = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "set").mockResolvedValue(row({ key: "plan", name: "Plan", source: "bundled", enabled: false }));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByTestId("skill-switch-plan"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(put).toHaveBeenCalledWith("plan", { enabled: false }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-switch-plan")).not.toBeChecked());
  });

  it("a dangerous skill has no switch: it turns on only after reading why", async () => {
    mount();
    await screen.findByTestId("skill-sneaky");
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("skill-switch-sneaky")).toBeNull();
    const anyway = screen.getByTestId("skill-turn-on-anyway-sneaky");
    expect(anyway).toBeDisabled();
    fireEvent.click(within(screen.getByTestId("skill-sneaky")).getByText("skillLibrary.readWhy"));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-findings-sneaky")).toHaveTextContent("Ignore all previous instructions");
    const put = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "set").mockResolvedValue(row({ key: "sneaky", source: "agents", enabled: true }));
    fireEvent.click(anyway);
    await waitFor(() => expect(put).toHaveBeenCalledWith("sneaky", { enabled: true, read_scan: true }));
  });

  it("a source switch writes through and reloads the list", async () => {
    mount();
    const setSource = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "setSource").mockResolvedValue(
      SOURCES.map((s) => (s.source === "claude" ? { ...s, on: true } : s)));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByTestId("skill-source-switch-claude"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(setSource).toHaveBeenCalledWith("claude", true));
    await waitFor(() => expect(skillsApi.list).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
  });

  it("pins a skill", async () => {
    mount();
    const put = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "set").mockResolvedValue(row({ key: "plan", source: "bundled", pinned: true }));
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByTestId("skill-pin-plan"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(put).toHaveBeenCalledWith("plan", { pinned: true }));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `web/src/api/skills.ts`:

```ts
import { request } from "./client";

/** Where a skill comes from (0.1.61 §C1). */
export type SkillSourceKind = "bundled" | "imported" | "saved" | "agents" | "claude" | "codex";
export interface SkillFinding { rule: string; level: string; file: string; line: number; excerpt: string }
/** One row of GET /skills (server/services/skill_library.py): one status per skill (§C6). */
export interface SkillRow {
  key: string; name: string; description: string; source: SkillSourceKind; enabled: boolean;
  has_scripts: boolean; local_only: boolean; license: string | null;
  scan: { verdict: "clean" | "caution" | "dangerous"; findings: SkillFinding[] } | null;
  invocable: boolean; pinned: boolean; declared: Record<string, unknown>; folder: string | null;
}
/** A folder other agents share (§C1): ~/.agents/skills, ~/.claude/skills, ~/.codex/skills. */
export interface SkillSourceView {
  source: "agents" | "claude" | "codex"; path: string; on: boolean; exists: boolean; found: number; shadowed: string[];
}

const json = (method: string, body: unknown) => ({ method, body: JSON.stringify(body) });

export const skillsApi = {
  list: () => request<SkillRow[]>("/skills"),
  set: (key: string, flags: { enabled?: boolean; pinned?: boolean; read_scan?: boolean }) =>
    request<SkillRow>(`/skills/${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, json("PUT", flags)),
  sources: () => request<SkillSourceView[]>("/skills/sources"),
  setSource: (source: SkillSourceView["source"], on: boolean) =>
    request<SkillSourceView[]>(`/skills/sources/${source}`, json("PUT", { on })),
  /** §C7: the person's answer to a draft from chat. */
  save: (candidateId: number) =>
    request<{ ok: boolean; key?: string; enabled?: boolean; reason?: string }>(`/skills/candidates/${candidateId}/promote`,
      { method: "POST" }),
  notNow: (candidateId: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/skills/candidates/${candidateId}/reject`, { method: "POST" }),
};
```

Create `web/src/components/skills/SkillLibrary.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { AlertTriangle, FileCode2, Lock, Pin, PinOff } from "lucide-react";
import { skillsApi, type SkillRow, type SkillSourceKind, type SkillSourceView } from "../../api/skills";
import EquipPopover from "../EquipPopover";
import { Button, Notice } from "../kit";

const ORDER: SkillSourceKind[] = ["bundled", "imported", "saved", "agents", "claude", "codex"];
type Flags = { enabled?: boolean; pinned?: boolean; read_scan?: boolean };

/**
 * The skills (0.1.61 §C1, §C6): grouped by where they come from. The folders other agents share
 * each have their switch. Every skill has one status: On/Off, "Has scripts", "Local use only"
 * and its scan note. A skill its scan turned off is turned on only after reading why. No
 * spawn-era tags.
 */
export default function SkillLibrary({ assign = false, refreshKey = 0 }: { assign?: boolean; refreshKey?: number }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<SkillRow[] | null>(null);
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<SkillSourceView[]>([]);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [list, folders] = await Promise.all([skillsApi.list(), skillsApi.sources()]);
      setRows(list); setSources(folders); setFailed(false);
    } catch { setFailed(true); }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, [load, refreshKey]);

  async function change(row: SkillRow, flags: Flags) {
    setBusy(row.key); setError(null);
    try {
      const next = await skillsApi.set(row.key, flags);
      setRows((old) => old?.map((r) => (r.key === row.key ? next : r)) ?? old);
    } catch { setError(t("skillLibrary.failed")); }
    finally { setBusy(null); }
  }

  async function flipSource(folder: SkillSourceView, on: boolean) {
    setBusy(`source:${folder.source}`); setError(null);
    try { setSources(await skillsApi.setSource(folder.source, on)); await load(); }
    catch { setError(t("skillLibrary.failed")); }
    finally { setBusy(null); }
  }

  if (failed) return <Notice tone="error" role="alert">{t("skillLibrary.loadFailed")}</Notice>;
  if (!rows) return null;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6" data-testid="skill-library">
      {error && <Notice tone="error" role="alert">{error}</Notice>}
      {rows.length === 0 && sources.length === 0 && <p className="text-[13px] text-subtle-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.empty")}</p>}
      {ORDER.map((source) => {
        const list = rows.filter((r) => r.source === source);
        const folder = sources.find((f) => f.source === source);
        if (!list.length && !folder) return null;
        return (
          <section key={source} data-testid={`skill-source-${source}`} className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 px-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">
              <span>{t(`skillLibrary.source_${source}`)}</span>
              {folder && (
                <input type="checkbox" role="switch" className="kit-switch ml-auto" aria-label={t(`skillLibrary.source_${source}`)}
                  data-testid={`skill-source-switch-${source}`} checked={folder.on} disabled={busy === `source:${source}`}
                  onChange={(event) => void flipSource(folder, event.target.checked)} />
              )}
            </h2>
            {folder && (
              <p className="px-1 text-[12px] text-subtle-foreground">
                {folder.exists ? t("skillLibrary.sourcePath", { path: folder.path }) : t("skillLibrary.sourceMissing", { path: folder.path })}
                {folder.shadowed.length > 0 && <> · {t("skillLibrary.shadowed", { names: folder.shadowed.join(", ") })}</>}
              </p>
            )}
            {list.length > 0 && (
              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface">
                {list.map((row) => (
                  <SkillLine key={row.key} row={row} busy={busy === row.key} assign={assign} open={open === row.key}
                    onOpen={() => setOpen(open === row.key ? null : row.key)} onChange={(flags) => void change(row, flags)} />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function SkillLine({ row, busy, assign, open, onOpen, onChange }: {
  row: SkillRow; busy: boolean; assign: boolean; open: boolean; onOpen: () => void; onChange: (flags: Flags) => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const dangerous = row.scan?.verdict === "dangerous";
  const rules = (row.scan?.findings ?? []).map((f) => f.rule).join(", ");
  const declared = Object.keys(row.declared ?? {});
  return (
    <div data-testid={`skill-${row.key}`} data-enabled={row.enabled}
      className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-border px-4 py-2.5 first:border-t-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5">
          <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[14px]">{row.name}
            {row.has_scripts && (
              <span data-testid={`skill-scripts-${row.key}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-fill px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground">
                <FileCode2 size={11} />{t("skillLibrary.hasScripts")}</span>
            )}
            {row.local_only && (
              <span data-testid={`skill-local-${row.key}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-fill px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground">
                <Lock size={11} />{t("skillLibrary.localOnly")}</span>
            )}
            {!row.invocable && <span className="text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.hidden")}</span>}
          </span>
          <span className="line-clamp-2 text-[12px] text-subtle-foreground">{row.description}</span>
          {row.scan && row.scan.verdict !== "clean" && (
            <span data-testid={`skill-scan-${row.key}`} className={`flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] ${dangerous ? "text-danger" : "text-warning"}`}>
              <AlertTriangle size={12} />{t(dangerous ? "skillLibrary.scanDangerous" : "skillLibrary.scanCaution", { rules })}
              <button type="button" className="underline" aria-expanded={open} onClick={onOpen}>{t("skillLibrary.readWhy")}</button>
            </span>
          )}
          {declared.length > 0 && (
            <span className="text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.declared", { items: declared.join(", ") })}</span>
          )}
        </div>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={row.pinned} data-testid={`skill-pin-${row.key}`} disabled={busy}
          title={t(row.pinned ? "skillLibrary.unpin" : "skillLibrary.pin")} aria-label={t(row.pinned ? "skillLibrary.unpin" : "skillLibrary.pin")}
          onClick={() => onChange({ pinned: !row.pinned })} className="shrink-0 text-subtle-foreground hover:text-foreground">
          {row.pinned ? <PinOff size={14} /> : <Pin size={14} />}
        </button>
        {assign && <EquipPopover kind="skill" capKey={row.key} />}
        {dangerous && !row.enabled ? (
          <Button size="sm" tone="destructive" data-testid={`skill-turn-on-anyway-${row.key}`} disabled={busy || !open}
            onClick={() => onChange({ enabled: true, read_scan: true })}>{t("skillLibrary.turnOnAnyway")}</Button>
        ) : (
          <input type="checkbox" role="switch" className="kit-switch" aria-label={row.name} data-testid={`skill-switch-${row.key}`}
            checked={row.enabled} disabled={busy} onChange={(event) => onChange({ enabled: event.target.checked })} />
        )}
      </div>
      {open && row.scan && (
        <ul data-testid={`skill-findings-${row.key}`} className="ml-1 flex flex-col gap-0.5 text-[11px] text-subtle-foreground">
          {row.scan.findings.map((f, i) => (
            <li key={i}><span className="font-mono">{f.file}:{f.line}</span> {f.rule} — {f.excerpt}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/** 0.1.61 §C7: a skill Arslan drafted in chat is a card — Save as a skill / Not now — and nothing
 *  is saved until the person clicks. */
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor, cleanup } from "@testing-library/react";

vi.mock("react-i18next", () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (k: string, o?: Record<string, unknown>) => (o ? `${k}:${Object.values(o).join("|")}` : k) }),
}));

import SkillProposalCard from "../components/skills/SkillProposalCard";
import { skillsApi } from "../api/skills";
import { initialArslanState, useArslanStore } from "../stores/arslanStore";
import { toUiMessages } from "../api/adapters";

const PROPOSAL = { candidateId: 7, key: "weekly-report", name: "Weekly report", description: "Turn the CSV into a report.",
  body: "## Trigger\nWeekly numbers.", files: ["scripts/report.py"] };

afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.restoreAllMocks(); });

describe("skill proposal", () => {
  it("the skill_proposed frame becomes a card in the conversation", () => {
    useArslanStore.setState(initialArslanState());
    useArslanStore.getState().handleFrame({ type: "skill_proposed", candidate_id: 7, key: "weekly-report",
      name: "Weekly report", description: "Turn the CSV into a report.", body: "## Trigger\nWeekly numbers.",
      files: ["scripts/report.py"] });
    const items = useArslanStore.getState().items;
    expect(items[items.length - 1]).toMatchObject({ kind: "skill", skillProposal: PROPOSAL });
    expect(toUiMessages(items).at(-1)?.skillProposal).toMatchObject({ candidateId: 7 });
  });

  it("shows the draft and saves it only on Save as a skill", async () => {
    const save = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "save").mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, key: "weekly-report", enabled: true });
    render(<SkillProposalCard proposal={PROPOSAL} />);
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("ask-countdown")).toBeNull();               // a proposal does not expire (Ruling I)
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-proposal-body")).toHaveTextContent("Weekly numbers.");
    expect(screen.getByTestId("skill-proposal")).toHaveTextContent("scripts/report.py");
    expect(save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("skill-proposal-save"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(7));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("skill-proposal-decided")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.proposalSaved");
  });

  it("keys typed anywhere never answer it: only a click saves", async () => {
    const save = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "save").mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, key: "weekly-report", enabled: true });
    const notNow = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "notNow").mockResolvedValue({ ok: true });
    render(<SkillProposalCard proposal={PROPOSAL} />);
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "Enter", metaKey: true });
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "Escape" });
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));
    expect(save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(notNow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a draft its scan turned off says so; Not now leaves it unsaved", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "save").mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, key: "weekly-report", enabled: false });
    render(<SkillProposalCard proposal={PROPOSAL} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("skill-proposal-save"));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("skill-proposal-decided")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.proposalSavedOff");
    cleanup();
    const notNow = vi.spyOn(skillsApi, "notNow").mockResolvedValue({ ok: true });
    render(<SkillProposalCard proposal={PROPOSAL} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("skill-proposal-not-now"));
    await waitFor(() => expect(notNow).toHaveBeenCalledWith(7));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId("skill-proposal-decided")).toHaveTextContent("skillLibrary.proposalDismissed");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`web/src/lib/startChat.ts`:

```ts
/** 0.1.61 §C7: open a new conversation with a draft typed in its composer. Nothing is sent on the
 *  person's behalf — they finish the sentence and send it. */
export const START_CHAT_EVENT = "arslan:start-chat";

export function startChat(prefill: string): void {
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(START_CHAT_EVENT, { detail: { prefill } }));
}

/** App's listener: a fresh conversation id, the draft in its composer, then open it. */
export function startChatHandler(open: (conversationId: string) => void, drafts: Map<string, string>) {
  return (event: Event) => {
    const prefill = (event as CustomEvent<{ prefill?: string }>).detail?.prefill ?? "";
    const conversationId = `thread-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    if (prefill) drafts.set(conversationId, prefill);
    open(conversationId);
  };
}
```

`web/src/components/skills/SkillProposalCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import type { SkillProposal } from "../../api/client.types";
import { skillsApi } from "../../api/skills";
import { AskCard, Notice } from "../kit";

/**
 * "Save as a skill?" (0.1.61 §C7): a method Arslan drafted in this conversation, answered in place
 * like a plan proposal. Nothing is saved until "Save as a skill"; "Not now" leaves it unsaved.
 */
export default function SkillProposalCard({ proposal }: { proposal: SkillProposal }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [state, setState] = useState<"open" | "busy" | "saved" | "savedOff" | "dismissed">("open");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function answer(save: boolean) {
    setState("busy"); setError(null);
    try {
      if (save) {
        const out = await skillsApi.save(proposal.candidateId);
        if (!out.ok) throw new Error(out.reason ?? "not saved");
        setState(out.enabled === false ? "savedOff" : "saved");
      } else {
        await skillsApi.notNow(proposal.candidateId);
        setState("dismissed");
      }
    } catch {
      setError(t("skillLibrary.failed"));
      setState("open");
    }
  }

  if (state === "saved" || state === "savedOff" || state === "dismissed") {
    const key = state === "saved" ? "skillLibrary.proposalSaved"
      : state === "savedOff" ? "skillLibrary.proposalSavedOff" : "skillLibrary.proposalDismissed";
    return <p className="px-1 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground" data-testid="skill-proposal-decided">{t(key)}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <AskCard testId="skill-proposal" who={t("skillLibrary.proposalWho")}
        title={t("skillLibrary.proposalTitle", { name: proposal.name })} said={proposal.description || null}
        detail={(
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <pre data-testid="skill-proposal-body"
              className="max-h-48 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-background p-2 font-mono text-[11px]">
              {proposal.body}
            </pre>
            {proposal.files.length > 0 && (
              <p className="text-[12px] text-subtle-foreground">{t("skillLibrary.proposalFiles", { files: proposal.files.join(", ") })}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        allowLabel={t("skillLibrary.proposalSave")} declineLabel={t("skillLibrary.proposalNotNow")}
        busy={state === "busy"} onAllow={() => void answer(true)} onDecline={() => void answer(false)}
        // Not a blocking card: the turn goes on beside it, so ⌘⏎ and Esc typed anywhere must never
        // answer it — saving a skill is a click on "Save as a skill", nothing else (0.1.57 rule).
        keys={false}
        allowTestId="skill-proposal-save" declineTestId="skill-proposal-not-now" />
      {error && <Notice tone="error">{error}</Notice>}
    </div>
  );
}
```
